# Good Egg (EggStarGalaxy) sound inventory

Generated by `inv.py` (scratchpad) from vendor/Petari and the disc (data/fs/files). Scope: every placement name in
EggStarGalaxy.arc and the zones its ZoneList loads (out/sets.json "EggStar", all layers, so every scenario incl. the
comets), mapped to classes through NameObjFactory's cCreateTable (template `createNameObj<X>` and `MR::createX`
functions), plus every class those files `new` (transitively, as tools/sfxcut.py does; DinoPackunVs2 and AudioLib left
out). Then global code: Mario, talk, HUD / screen sequences, pickups, utilities. Line numbers are Petari's
(`src/Game/` omitted). "function" is the enclosing definition, "guard" the nearest enclosing if / case / else
(automatic: read the source for anything subtle). Names in `<...>` are variables / class helpers (see the source
line); `literal` rows are SE names in tables or variables, not directly at a call.

Contents: 0 summary / mechanics, 1 placed classes, 2 global code, 3 Mario's sound table, 4 talk sounds,
5 animation sound tables (.bas), 6 deduplicated names.

## 0. Summary and mechanics

### Counts

- 231 placement names, 103 classes; 245 .cpp files reached, 112 of them with sound calls (section 1: ~870 call rows),
  plus 57 global files (section 2: ~370 rows).
- 771 distinct names in total: OBJECT 149, PLAYER_MOTION 134, ENEMY_MOTION 106, SYSTEM 80, PLAYER_VOICE 71,
  SUPPORTER_VOICE 42, BOSS_MOTION 40, BOSS_VOICE 33, SUPPORTER_MOTION 33, ENEMY_VOICE 8, DEMO 6, REMIX_SEQ 3,
  ATMOSPHERE 1; 64 non-SE names (BGM_* / MBGM_* stage music, ME_* music effects, CS_* Wii remote speaker sounds);
  1 name the BSTN table lacks (SE_BM_BUTLER_ABSORB, Tico.cpp). Categories are the BSTN groups of AudioRes/SMR.szs
  (tools/audio.py se_names).
- This is a superset: it counts all 205 entries of Mario's table (bee / boo / spring / fire / ice modes too; Good Egg
  does place MorphItemObjHopper and MorphItemObjIce), every scenario's actors, Luigi's event, the scenario select and
  result screens.
- 101 .bas files in 20 archives the galaxy loads (EggStar ObjectData + resident ones); 79 names appear only in .bas
  tables, never in code.

### Notable

- **Mario's sounds are table-driven, not code-driven.** Player code calls `playSound("<JP key>")` (MarioModule::
  playSound -> Mario::playSoundJ, MarioSound.cpp:1742). The key is looked up in `soundlist` (MarioSound.cpp:29, 205
  entries: JP key, SE id, flags, optional companion key). Flags byte 0 & 3 picks the call: 0 MR::startSound on Mario,
  1 MR::startLevelSound, 2 MR::startSystemSE, 3 MR::startSystemLevelSE; flags & 0xC (4 / 8) also plays the companion
  key when under water. After that, `"声" + key` (voice) is looked up too and played as a one-shot if present, so
  `playSound("中ジャンプ")` plays both the motion SE and SE_PV_JUMP_M. Unknown keys are silently ignored (listed under
  section 3). The `timing` argument (usually -1) goes to the level-A parameter (see SoundUtil below).
- **Footsteps are animation-driven.** Mario's footsteps, crawls, pole climbs, sleep voices and the power-star-get voice
  are in MarioAnime.arc's 39 .bas files (walk / run / runslope / squatwalk / sleep / powerstarget...), started by
  Mario::startBas from MarioAnimator (MarioAnimator.cpp:362-528, 877-922) and MarioWalk.cpp:380 ("RunSlope"), 435
  ("DamageWait"). The ground's sound code (KCL attribute -> MR::getMapSoundCodeFoot, SoundUtil.cpp:262) selects the
  surface variant: AudSoundObject::setMapCodeToPort (AudSoundObject.cpp:302-332) writes port 12 = (code-1)/7 and
  port 11 = ((code-1)%7)*12 into SE_PM_HIPDROP, FALLDOWN_S/M, FOOTNOTE_L/R, FOOTNOTE_SUB_L/R, JUMP, LAND, HAND,
  LAND_HEAVY; the sequence reads those ports, so one SE name stands for several waves (grass, stone, sand, water...).
  Mario's dark SE version drops FALLDOWN / FOOTNOTE (AudAnmSoundObject::getSoundID, AudAnmSoundObject.cpp:39-94),
  and SE_PM_STAR_POWER_GET_1 from a .bas is always dropped (-1) there.
- **Every actor's BCK can carry sound.** MR::startBck / startBckWithInterpole / startBckNoInterpole /
  startBckAtFirstStep / tryStartBck / startBckIfExist all call MR::startBas(actor, bckName) (LiveActorUtil.cpp:
  977-1009, 1393); MR::actorSoundMovement (from LiveActor::movement, LiveActor.cpp:154) feeds the BCK frame to the
  animator every frame. Only actors that created a sound object (initSound) play it. Good Egg's: DinoPackun (14 .bas:
  footsteps, shouts, tail), Kinopio (7), CocoSambo body / head, Kuribo / KuriboMini steps, Karipon, OtaKing, PackunPetit,
  Takobo, Tico / TicoBaby / TicoFat / SpinTico, PackunFlower, GrandStar (resident, not Good Egg's).
- **Level sounds** (`startLevelSound`, names usually `_LV_`) must be called every frame: JAUSoundObject::
  startLevelSound reuses the handle already playing that id and refreshes its life time (JAudio2, not decompiled in
  Petari; behaviour as in the Twilight Princess decomp); it stops on its own a frame after the calls stop, unless
  the last argument (param5 > 0) sets a longer life time.
- **Object-name tables**: MapObj/StageEffectDataTable.cpp gives start / moving (level) / stop SEs per object name for
  generic movers (AnmModelObj, RailMoveObj, SideSpikeMoveStep, propellers): see the end of section 1. Also its
  isRiddleSeTypeStop -> SE_SY_READ_RIDDLE_S.
- **Talk**: TalkStateShort::open / TalkStateEvent::open (TalkState.cpp:73, 97) call MR::startTalkSound(message info
  byte 6) -> AudTalkSoundData cTalkSoundList (0 = none). The BMG INF1 entries (tools/msg.py info()[2]) give the number
  per message: section 4.
- **CS_* sounds** go to the Wii remote speaker (MR::startCSSound): when the speaker is not playable the second
  argument (an SE name or null) is played as a system SE instead. On PS2: play that fallback or nothing.
- startSystemSE("SE_SY_READ_RIDDLE_S") is skipped while the player is dead (SoundUtil.cpp:636-640).
- Star-pointer and pickup sounds are in global code: startDPDHitSound (ME_DPD_HIT or SE_SY_DPD_HIT),
  startDPDFreezeLevelSound (SE_EM_LV_DPD_POINT), limitedStarPieceHitSound (registers SE_OJ_STAR_PIECE_HIT_ENEMY as a
  limited sound, 1 at a time), Coin.cpp, StarPiece*.cpp, PowerStar.cpp, StarPointerLayout / Guidance.
- "ME" (music effects: ME_DPD_HIT, ME_2P_*) only play when the stage has a chord table loaded (MR::hasME); otherwise
  the SE fallback plays.

### SoundUtil (Util/SoundUtil.cpp, include/Game/Util/SoundUtil.hpp)

Every name is turned into a JAISoundID by AudSoundNameConverter (the BSTN name table). Actor sounds go through
`pActor->mSoundObject` (an AudAnmSoundObject: positional, with handles and the .bas animator); system / atmosphere
sounds through global sound objects (AudWrap::getSystemSeObject / getAtmosphereSeObject: not positional).

| function | what it does |
|---|---|
| `startSystemSE(name, p2 = -1, p3 = -1)` | system object, startSoundParam(id, p2, p3) |
| `startSystemLevelSE(name, p2 = -1, p3 = -1)` | system object, startLevelSoundParam |
| `stopSystemSE(name, fade = 0)` | stop it, fade frames |
| `startAtmosphereSE / startAtmosphereLevelSE(name, p2, p3)` | ambience object, same as the system pair |
| `startSoundObject(obj, name)` / `startSoundObjectLevel(obj, name, life = -1)` / `startSoundObjectLevelParam(obj, name, a, b, life)` | on an explicit AudSoundObject; life > 0 -> updateLifeTime(life) |
| `startSound(actor, name, p3 = -1, p4 = -1)` | sets the map code from the actor's Binder ground, then p3 == -1 ? startSound(id) : startSoundParam(id, p3, p4) |
| `startSoundSeVer(actor, name, p3, p4)` | same after convertSoundIdFromSeVersion (an actor's alternate set, e.g. water) |
| `startLevelSound(actor, name, p3 = -1, p4 = -1, p5 = -1)` | map code, then p3 == -1 ? startSoundObjectLevel(obj, name, p5) : startSoundObjectLevelParam(obj, name, p3, p4, p5) |
| `startLevelSoundSeVer` | as above with the SE version |
| `stopSound(actor, name, fade = 0)` / `releaseSoundHandle` / `setSeVersion` | |
| `startBas(actor, bckName, reversed, loopStart, loopEnd)` | the .bas of that name from the actor's archive (if any) -> AudAnmSoundObject::startAnimation |
| `actorSoundMovement(actor)` | per frame: map code, update(BCK frame) when the model has BCKs, process() |
| `getMapSoundCodeFoot(actor)` | ground (else roof, else wall) triangle's sound code attribute, -1 none |
| `startTalkSound(no, actor)` | cTalkSoundList[no]; actor null -> system SE |
| `startSystemME(name)` | music effect, only if hasME() |
| `startCSSound(cs, seFallback, p)` | Wii remote speaker, else startSystemSE(seFallback) |
| `startSpinHitSound` / `startBlowHitSound` / `startDPDHitSound` / `startDPDFreezeLevelSound` / `limitedStarPieceHitSound` / `start2P*Sound` | fixed names, see Notable |
| `limitedSound(name, n)` | registers a voice limit for that id |
| `startStageBGM / startSubBGM / startBossBGM / startEventBGM / startAfterBossBGM / startCurrentStageBGM` | music (MBGM_* names; stage BGM from AudStageBgmTable by stage + scenario) |
| `submitSE / permitSE` (Trig / Level) | mute gates: while set, startSound / startLevelSound refuse everything but SE_SYSTEM / HOME menu groups |

**The -1 arguments.** For one-shots and system SEs the two extra ints are "levels" A and B passed to
AudSoundObject::startSoundParam / startLevelSoundParam (AudSoundObject.cpp:141-166), which hands them to three
per-sound switch tables, modifySe_Kawamura / _Takezawa / _Gohara (AudSoundObject_*.cpp): only listed ids use them, each
in its own way (e.g. SE_OJ_COIN_BOUND: volume = A / 100; SE_OJ_KEY_SWITCH_BOUND: (A / 100)^2; rolling spheres: volume
from a speed; boss bug: volume A / 1000 and B written to port 10). -1 in the first slot means "no parameters": the
plain startSound / startLevelSound path, nothing modified. For level sounds the fifth argument is a life time in
frames (only when > 0). Mario's table passes its `timing` as A, and playSoundTrampleCombo passes the combo count
(SE_SY_TRAMPLE_COMBO, A = 0..6). Ids with no case keep volume / pitch 1.

### .bas format (JAUSoundAnimation, libs/JSystem/include/JSystem/JAudio2/JAUSoundAnimator.hpp)

Big-endian. `u16 count`, 2 pad, `u32 control` (0 on disc, a runtime pointer), then `count` x 0x20-byte
JAUSoundAnimationSound sorted by frame:

| off | type | field |
|---|---|---|
| 0x00 | u32 | sound id {u8 section (0 = SE), u8 group (BSTN category), u16 index} |
| 0x04 | f32 | note-on frame |
| 0x08 | f32 | note-off frame (== on: one-shot at that frame) |
| 0x0C | f32 | base pitch |
| 0x10 | u32 | flags: 1 forward only, 2 reverse only, 4 stop when the animation changes, 8 play once, 0x10 stop at note-off, 0x20 stop when the speed is 0, 0x40 life time = on..off (a level sound held over the range), 0x80 repeat at intervals |
| 0x14 | u8 | base volume (0..127) |
| 0x15 | s8 | pitch delta |
| 0x16 | u8 | play time |
| 0x17 | u8 | base pan |
| 0x18 | s8 | volume delta |
| 0x19 | u8 | repeat interval |
| 0x1A | s8, u32 @0x1C | unknown |

Loaded by ResourceHolder (".bas" res table, ResourceHolder.cpp:22, 90, 201) under the file name (the BCK's name).
AudAnmSoundObject::update(frame) -> JAUSoundAnimator::updateAnimSound plays the entries whose note-on frame was
crossed (direction by `reversed`, loop range from setLoopFrame); modifySoundParams clamps the speed to 3. The archive
check above (python, budget.rarc_files + yaz0) found them in 20 of the loaded archives; tools/sfxcut.py bas_ids()
already reads the id word.

## 3. Mario's sound table (Player/MarioSound.cpp `soundlist`: JP key -> SE id; Mario::playSoundJ)

kind: flags byte 0 & 3: 0 one-shot on Mario, 1 level on Mario, 2 system SE, 3 system level SE; flags & 0xC (4 / 8): also plays the entry named in column "+water" when under water. A key with no caller below can still be played: playSoundJ("X") also plays "声X" (voice) if it exists; names also come from variables (MarioAnimator, MarioActor tables).

## 5. Animation sound tables (.bas) in the archives Good Egg loads (EggStar ObjectData + resident)

Played by MR::startBas(actor, bckName) from every MR::startBck* (LiveActorUtil.cpp:977-1009, 1393), Mario by Mario::startBas from MarioAnimator; MR::actorSoundMovement (LiveActor::movement, LiveActor.cpp:154) feeds the BCK frame to AudAnmSoundObject::update -> JAUSoundAnimator::updateAnimSound.

Columns: sound, note-on frame, note-off frame, flags (1 fwd only, 2 rev only, 4 stop on anim change, 8 once, 0x10 stop at note-off, 0x20 stop at speed 0, 0x40 life time on..off, 0x80 repeat), volume, pitch.

- spin.bas: `SE_SM_TICO_SPIN` 0-0 f00 v127 p1
